Serve the decoupled PP4 Prefill once: 3 x PP4/TP1/EP1, direct last-stage
results on a dedicated gloo group (publisher thread), in-stream PyNccl
proxies on the forward stream, consensus lag 2, async depth 2, layers
24/24/24/22, concurrency 235; Decode unchanged (TP4/DP4/EP4, EAGLE3 3/1/4
FP8 draft, cap 176). Image 40742736653 unchanged, runtime overlays only
(pp4-direct-result.patch, pp4-proxy-instream.patch appended to the 17
existing ones).

Why: the profile of run 40796263174 (research note
2026-10-29-pp4-profile-critical-path.md) showed stage compute of 109-113 ms
per 2-request microbatch, 10% below the EP4 equivalent, but a 164 ms period:
stages 0-2 idled 52-56 ms in NCCL SendRecv and the last stage's MegaMoE
stalled 37-66 ms per microbatch under a co-resident NCCL recv kernel, while
the scheduler spent 77% of each period in pp.wait_result because the upstream
loop received the output ring before launching the next forward. The ten
preceding commits remove that coupling.

CPU gate: the full 19-overlay chain applies on the reconstructed fc275674
tree, py_compile of 26 patched files, the PP4 CPU checks and the new
four-stage loop harness (scripts/check-pp4-direct-loop.py, real source,
96 iterations) pass, full suite 976 passed / 77 skipped. The system model
predicts 9,217-9,351 if the stage period is 113.5-120.5 ms and below 9,235
if it exceeds about 123 ms; c215 would predict 9,008-9,158. Nothing is
verified on GPU: in-stream NCCL waits, the gloo result group, the publisher
thread, the NIXL path and numerical agreement are all first exercised here.

Judge by the PP0 issue interval (target <= 120 ms, was 164), the absence of
NCCL/MegaMoE overlap and of stage-3 MegaMoE stalls in the profile pass,
Prefill GPU power near the 690 W cap, start-burst-only TTFT failures and
ITL p99 below 20 ms. Expected timeline: READY about 290 s, benchmark 586 s,
profile pass within the 17-minute limit.
